a satellite map/dashboard.\\n\\nInstead, build a product experience where the user first understands SKYGUARD XAI and then actively checks a satellite.\\n\\nThe primary interaction should     ↓\\nNORMAL / WARNING / HIGH RISK / CRITICAL\\n      ↓\\nDETAILED SATELLITE INTELLIGENCE\\n\\nThe application should feel like:\\n\\n\\\"Enter a satellite → SKYGUARD XAI analyzes its current condition → tells you whether it is safe or at risk → explains WHY.\\\"\\n\\nThe map is supporting information, not the main product.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 1. Before Risk Becomes Impact.\\n\\nSupporting text:\\n\\n\u003e SKYGUARD XAI continuously monitors satellite telemetry, detects abnormal behavior, identifies potential space-object risks, and converts live space data into explainable risk intelligence.\\n\\nDo NOT make the landing page overly decorative.\\n\\nIt should look like a serious AI intelligence platform.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 2. SKYGUARD XAI — FRONTEND REDESIGN PROMPT\\n\\n## Objective\\n\\nThe current SKYGUARD XAI frontend is NOT acceptable in its current form.\\n\\nRight now, it looks primarily like a map showcasing satellites/objects.\\n\\nThe user cannot immediately understand:\\n\\n- What SKYGUARD XAI is doing\\n- What is happening right now\\n- Which satellite has a problem\\n- What the AI detected\\n- Why the satellite is considered risky\\n- What changed\\n- How serious the situation is\\n- What evidence caused the risk\\n- What the operator should look at next\\n\\nThe frontend must therefore be redesigned as an:\\n\\n# REAL-TIME SPACE INTELLIGENCE COMMAND CENTER\\n\\nThe map is only ONE component.\\n\\nThe main purpose of the frontend is to communicate:\\n\\nSPACE EVENT\\n→ AI ANALYSIS\\n→ RISK\\n→ EVIDENCE\\n→ ALERT\\n→ OPERATOR UNDERSTANDING\\n\\nDo NOT simply make the map bigger.\\n\\nDo NOT add random charts.\\n\\nDo NOT make this look like a generic admin dashboard.\\n\\nThe frontend should visually explain the intelligence pipeline.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 1. CORE FRONTEND MESSAGE\\n\\nWhen someone opens SKYGUARD XAI, they should understand the product within 5–10 seconds.\\n\\nThe first screen should answer:\\n\\n### WHAT IS HAPPENING?\\n\\nExample:\\n\\n\u003e 🚨 HIGH SPACE RISK DETECTED\\n\\n### WHERE?\\n\\n\u003e Satellite SAT-1042\\n\\n### WHAT DID AI DETECT?\\n\\n\u003e Abnormal telemetry + possible conjunction\\n\\n### HOW SERIOUS?\\n\\n\u003e 89% Risk\\n\\n### WHY?\\n\\n\u003e Temperature spike  \\n\u003e Power consumption increase  \\n\u003e Battery degradation  \\n\u003e 0.72 km estimated miss distance\\n\\n### WHAT SHOULD I DO?\\n\\n\u003e Operator review required\\n\\nThe UI must communicate this without requiring the user to explore five different pages.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 2. NEW DASHBOARD STRUCTURE\\n\\nRedesign the main dashboard into these sections:\\n\\n--------------------------------------------------\\nTOP NAVIGATION\\n--------------------------------------------------\\n\\nSKYGUARD XAI\\n\\nReal-Time Space Intelligence\\n\\n[ LIVE ● ]\\n\\nNavigation:\\n\\nOverview\\nSatellites\\nAlerts\\nEvents\\nAnalytics\\n\\nRight side:\\n\\nSystem Status:\\n● Operational\\n\\nData:\\nLIVE\\n\\nLast update:\\n2 sec ago\\n\\n--------------------------------------------------\\nPRIMARY INTELLIGENCE BANNER\\n--------------------------------------------------\\n\\nThis should be the most important area after the header.\\n\\nExample normal state:\\n\\nSPACE ENVIRONMENT\\nNo critical threats detected\\n\\nExample abnormal state:\\n\\n🚨 HIGH SPACE RISK DETECTED\\n\\nSAT-1042\\n\\nAI detected abnormal satellite telemetry\\nand elevated conjunction risk.\\n\\nRisk Score\\n89%\\n\\nConfidence\\n94%\\n\\nStatus\\nHIGH RISK\\n\\n[ View Intelligence ]\\n\\nThis immediately tells the user what SKYGUARD XAI is doing.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 3. DO NOT MAKE THE MAP THE HERO\\n\\nThe map should NOT occupy 70–80% of the screen.\\n\\nInstead use a balanced layout.\\n\\nSuggested structure:\\n\\n--------------------------------------------------\\n| HEADER                                         |\\n--------------------------------------------------\\n| INTELLIGENCE ALERT / CURRENT SITUATION         |\\n--------------------------------------------------\\n|                                                |\\n| RISK SUMMARY | SPACE MAP | LIVE EVENTS         |\\n|                                                |\\n--------------------------------------------------\\n| SATELLITE HEALTH | TELEMETRY | AI EVIDENCE     |\\n--------------------------------------------------\\n| ACTIVE ALERTS / CONJUNCTIONS                   |\\n--------------------------------------------------\\n\\nThe map should support the intelligence story.\\n\\nIt should not BE the story.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 4. SPACE MAP\\n\\nKeep the map, but make it meaningful.\\n\\nThe map should show:\\n\\n🛰 Satellite\\n○ Debris\\n⚠ High-risk object\\n🔴 Critical object\\n\\nUse clear visual states.\\n\\nWhen a satellite is normal:\\n\\nSAT-1001\\nNORMAL\\n\\nWhen risky:\\n\\nSAT-1042\\nHIGH RISK\\n\\nWhen the user selects SAT-1042:\\n\\n- highlight satellite\\n- show its trajectory\\n- highlight nearby object\\n- draw conjunction relationship\\n- show approximate closest approach\\n- show risk level\\n\\nExample:\\n\\n             DEB-2098\\n                 ○\\n                 |\\n                 | 0.72 km\\n                 |\\n                 ⚠\\n              SAT-1042\\n\\nThe map must explain WHY two objects are being highlighted.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 5. MAP LEGEND\\n\\nAdd a clear legend:\\n\\nSPACE OBJECTS\\n\\n🛰 Satellite\\n○ Debris\\n🟡 Warning\\n🟠 High Risk\\n🔴 Critical\\n\\nAlso show:\\n\\nLIVE TRACKING\\n\\nData age:\\n2 seconds\\n\\nThis makes the map understandable.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 6. SATELLITE OVERVIEW CARDS\\n\\nBelow/next to the map, display important satellite states.\\n\\nExample:\\n\\nSAT-1042\\n\\nHIGH RISK\\n\\nHealth\\n82%\\n\\nRisk\\n89%\\n\\nTemperature\\n71°C\\n\\nBattery\\n42%\\n\\nSignal\\n61%\\n\\nUpdated\\n2 sec ago\\n\\n[ Inspect ]\\n\\nDo NOT force the user to click into a page just to understand the basic situation.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 7. \\\"WHAT AI DETECTED\\\" SECTION\\n\\nThis is one of the most important changes.\\n\\nCreate a dedicated card:\\n\\n## WHAT AI DETECTED\\n\\nFor example:\\n\\n🚨 Telemetry Anomaly\\n\\nTemperature increased significantly above\\nnormal baseline.\\n\\nEvidence:\\n\\nTemperature\\n24°C → 71°C\\n\\nPower\\n62 → 94\\n\\nBattery\\n91% → 42%\\n\\nSignal\\n96% → 61%\\n\\nAI confidence:\\n\\n94%\\n\\nModel:\\n\\ntelemetry-anomaly-v1\\n\\n[ View telemetry ]\\n\\nThis section should visually connect raw telemetry to AI intelligence.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 8. AI REASONING / EVIDENCE\\n\\nDo not show only:\\n\\nRisk = 89%\\n\\nThat is not enough.\\n\\nShow:\\n\\n## WHY IS THIS RISKY?\\n\\n1. Temperature anomaly\\n   + High deviation from baseline\\n\\n2. Power anomaly\\n   + Sudden increase detected\\n\\n3. Battery degradation\\n   + Significant decline\\n\\n4. Conjunction detected\\n   + Estimated miss distance: 0.72 km\\n\\nThen:\\n\\nOVERALL RISK\\n\\n89%\\n\\nCONFIDENCE\\n\\n94%\\n\\nThis makes the system explainable.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 9. RISK BREAKDOWN\\n\\nCreate a visual risk breakdown.\\n\\nExample:\\n\\nOVERALL SPACE RISK\\n\\n89%\\n\\nTelemetry Anomaly\\n████████████ 35%\\n\\nSatellite Health\\n████████ 24%\\n\\nConjunction Risk\\n██████████ 30%\\n\\nOther\\n███ 11%\\n\\nThe exact percentages should come from the backend.\\n\\nDo NOT invent fake percentages just for UI.\\n\\nIf the backend currently does not provide contributors, create a clearly defined frontend contract and use actual backend values when available.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 10. LIVE EVENT TIMELINE\\n\\nCreate a prominent real-time event stream.\\n\\nTitle:\\n\\n## LIVE SPACE EVENTS\\n\\nExample:\\n\\n10:02:16\\n🚨 Conjunction detected\\n\\nSAT-1042\\n+\\nDEB-2098\\n\\nRisk:\\nHIGH\\n\\n---\\n\\n10:02:14\\n🤖 Anomaly detected\\n\\nSAT-1042\\n\\nTemperature anomaly detected.\\n\\n---\\n\\n10:02:13\\n⚠ Risk increased\\n\\n62% → 89%\\n\\n---\\n\\n10:02:11\\n📡 Telemetry received\\n\\nSAT-1042\\n\\n---\\n\\n10:02:08\\n🛰 Satellite position updated\\n\\nSAT-1042\\n\\nThe user should visually understand:\\n\\nDATA ARRIVED\\n→ AI ANALYZED\\n→ RISK CHANGED\\n→ ALERT GENERATED\\n\\nThis is essential for the Datathon theme:\\n\\nREAL-TIME AI FOR CRITICAL DECISIONS.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 11. REAL-TIME INDICATOR\\n\\nThe UI must clearly communicate that the system is live.\\n\\nAdd:\\n\\n● LIVE\\n\\nData stream:\\nCONNECTED\\n\\nEvents/sec:\\nactual backend value\\n\\nLast event:\\n2 sec ago\\n\\nProcessing:\\nACTIVE\\n\\nIf the backend is disconnected:\\n\\n○ DISCONNECTED\\n\\nData stream:\\nOFFLINE\\n\\nDo not pretend the system is live if it is not.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 12. SATELLITE DETAIL PAGE\\n\\nClicking a satellite should open a proper intelligence page.\\n\\nNot just another map.\\n\\nExample:\\n\\n==================================================\\nSAT-1042\\nSKYGUARD-1042\\n==================================================\\n\\nSTATUS:\\nHIGH RISK\\n\\nHEALTH:\\n82%\\n\\nRISK:\\n89%\\n\\nCONFIDENCE:\\n94%\\n\\nLAST UPDATE:\\n2 seconds ago\\n\\n==================================================\\nCURRENT SITUATION\\n==================================================\\n\\nAI has detected abnormal telemetry and\\nan elevated conjunction risk.\\n\\n==================================================\\nTELEMETRY\\n==================================================\\n\\nTemperature graph\\n\\nBattery graph\\n\\nPower graph\\n\\nSignal graph\\n\\nVelocity graph\\n\\nClearly highlight abnormal periods.\\n\\nExample:\\n\\n24°C ── 25 ── 26 ── 27 ── 71°C\\n                         ↑\\n                      ANOMALY\\n\\n==================================================\\nAI DETECTION\\n==================================================\\n\\nAnomaly:\\nHIGH\\n\\nEvidence:\\n\\nTemperature spike\\nPower increase\\nBattery degradation\\nSignal degradation\\n\\nModel:\\ntelemetry-anomaly-v1\\n\\nConfidence:\\n94%\\n\\n==================================================\\nCONJUNCTION\\n==================================================\\n\\nObject:\\nDEB-2098\\n\\nClosest approach:\\n0.72 km\\n\\nTime to closest approach:\\n18 minutes\\n\\nRisk:\\nHIGH\\n\\nIMPORTANT:\\n\\nThis is a decision-support warning.\\n\\nShow:\\n\\n\\\"Operator review required\\\"\\n\\nDo NOT show an automatic spacecraft maneuver command.\\n\\n==================================================\\nRISK HISTORY\\n==================================================\\n\\nLOW\\n ↓\\nMEDIUM\\n ↓\\nHIGH\\n ↓\\nCRITICAL\\n\\nShow the risk progression over time.\\n\\n==================================================\\nDATA QUALITY\\n==================================================\\n\\nData freshness:\\n2 sec\\n\\nData quality:\\nGOOD\\n\\nProcessing latency:\\nactual value\\n\\n==================================================\\n\\n# 13. SATELLITES PAGE\\n\\nCreate a dedicated page:\\n\\n## SATELLITE FLEET\\n\\nTop summary:\\n\\nTotal satellites\\n10\\n\\nNormal\\n6\\n\\nWarning\\n2\\n\\nHigh Risk\\n1\\n\\nCritical\\n1\\n\\nThen table:\\n\\n| Satellite | Health | Risk | Temp | Battery | Signal | Status | Updated |\\n| SAT-1001 | 96% | 8% | 24°C | 92% | 97% | Normal | 2s |\\n| SAT-1002 | 91% | 18% | 29°C | 87% | 93% | Normal | 1s |\\n| SAT-1042 | 82% | 89% | 71°C | 42% | 61% | High Risk | 2s |\\n\\nRows should be clickable.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 14. ALERTS PAGE\\n\\nCreate:\\n\\n## ACTIVE ALERTS\\n\\nEach alert should explain:\\n\\nWHAT\\nWHERE\\nWHY\\nRISK\\nWHEN\\n\\nExample:\\n\\n🚨 HIGH SPACE RISK\\n\\nSAT-1042\\n\\nTelemetry anomaly detected\\n\\nRisk:\\n89%\\n\\nConfidence:\\n94%\\n\\nReason:\\nTemperature and power deviation\\n\\n2 minutes ago\\n\\n---\\n\\n🚨 CONJUNCTION ALERT\\n\\nSAT-1042\\n+\\nDEB-2098\\n\\nMiss distance:\\n0.72 km\\n\\nRisk:\\n84%\\n\\n18 minutes to closest approach\\n\\nOperator review required\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 15. EVENTS PAGE\\n\\nCreate an event explorer.\\n\\nFilters:\\n\\nEvent Type\\n- Telemetry\\n- Anomaly\\n- Conjunction\\n- Risk\\n- Alert\\n\\nSatellite\\n\\nRisk Level\\n\\nTime\\n\\nExample:\\n\\n10:02:16\\nCONJUNCTION\\nSAT-1042\\nDEB-2098\\nHIGH\\n\\n10:02:14\\nANOMALY\\nSAT-1042\\nHIGH\\n\\n10:02:13\\nRISK_CHANGE\\nSAT-1042\\n62 → 89%\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 16. VISUAL HIERARCHY\\n\\nThe UI must have a clear hierarchy.\\n\\nHighest priority:\\n\\n1. Critical alert\\n2. Risk score\\n3. What AI detected\\n4. Why it is risky\\n5. Current affected satellite/object\\n6. Real-time event timeline\\n7. Map\\n8. Detailed telemetry\\n9. Supporting information\\n\\nDo not give equal visual importance to everything.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 17. COLOR SYSTEM\\n\\nUse colors semantically.\\n\\nNormal:\\nneutral/green\\n\\nWarning:\\nyellow\\n\\nHigh:\\norange\\n\\nCritical:\\nred\\n\\nDo not use bright colors everywhere.\\n\\nColor should communicate severity.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 18. TOOLTIPS / EXPLANATIONS\\n\\nTechnical terms should be understandable.\\n\\nFor example:\\n\\n\\\"Conjunction\\\"\\n\\nTooltip:\\n\\n\\\"Two space objects are predicted to pass within\\na potentially dangerous distance.\\\"\\n\\n\\\"Risk Score\\\"\\n\\nTooltip:\\n\\n\\\"Estimated severity based on telemetry anomalies,\\nsatellite health, and conjunction indicators.\\\"\\n\\n\\\"Confidence\\\"\\n\\nTooltip:\\n\\n\\\"How confident the AI model is in its prediction\\nbased on available data.\\\"\\n\\n\\\"Data Age\\\"\\n\\nTooltip:\\n\\n\\\"Time since the latest telemetry event was received.\\\"\\n\\nThis is important because judges/users should not need aerospace expertise.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 19. EMPTY STATES\\n\\nIf there are no alerts:\\n\\n## SPACE ENVIRONMENT STABLE\\n\\nNo high-risk events detected.\\n\\n10 satellites monitored\\n0 active critical alerts\\n\\nDo not leave blank panels.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 20. LOADING STATES\\n\\nNever show an empty screen while loading.\\n\\nUse:\\n\\nLoading satellite telemetry...\\n\\nConnecting to real-time data stream...\\n\\nAnalyzing telemetry...\\n\\nDetecting conjunctions...\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 21. ERROR STATES\\n\\nIf real-time connection fails:\\n\\n⚠ DATA STREAM INTERRUPTED\\n\\nLast successful update:\\n32 seconds ago\\n\\nThe UI must NEVER falsely display:\\n\\nLIVE\\n\\nwhen the backend is disconnected.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 22. DEMO MODE\\n\\nCreate a visible:\\n\\nDEMO MODE\\n\\ntoggle/button.\\n\\nWhen enabled, the deterministic scenario should run:\\n\\nNORMAL SAT-1042\\n        ↓\\nTelemetry anomaly\\n        ↓\\nRisk increases\\n        ↓\\nDEB-2098 approaches\\n        ↓\\nConjunction detected\\n        ↓\\nHigh-risk alert\\n\\nThe frontend should visually show the entire story.\\n\\nThe judge should be able to understand the project simply by watching the dashboard.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 23. IMPORTANT: BUILD A STORY, NOT A COLLECTION OF COMPONENTS\\n\\nThe frontend must tell this story:\\n\\n### STEP 1 — MONITOR\\n\\nSKYGUARD XAI continuously receives satellite telemetry.\\n\\n↓\\n\\n### STEP 2 — DETECT\\n\\nAI notices something unusual.\\n\\n↓\\n\\n### STEP 3 — UNDERSTAND\\n\\nThe system identifies the evidence.\\n\\n↓\\n\\n### STEP 4 — ASSESS\\n\\nRisk score increases.\\n\\n↓\\n\\n### STEP 5 — CORRELATE\\n\\nAnother space object creates conjunction risk.\\n\\n↓\\n\\n### STEP 6 — ALERT\\n\\nOperator receives a real-time alert.\\n\\n↓\\n\\n### STEP 7 — REVIEW\\n\\nOperator can inspect the satellite and evidence.\\n\\nThis story must be visible in the UI.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 24. FRONTEND COMPONENT ARCHITECTURE\\n\\nCreate reusable components such as:\\n\\nDashboardHeader\\n\\nSystemStatus\\n\\nIntelligenceBanner\\n\\nRiskSummaryCard\\n\\nSatelliteStatusCard\\n\\nSpaceMap\\n\\nMapLegend\\n\\nLiveEventTimeline\\n\\nAIInsightCard\\n\\nEvidencePanel\\n\\nRiskBreakdown\\n\\nTelemetryChart\\n\\nConjunctionCard\\n\\nAlertCard\\n\\nDataFreshnessIndicator\\n\\nSatelliteTable\\n\\nSatelliteDetail\\n\\nEmptyState\\n\\nErrorState\\n\\nLoadingState\\n\\nDo not duplicate UI logic.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 25. RESPONSIVE DESIGN\\n\\nThe interface should work on:\\n\\nDesktop\\nLaptop\\nTablet\\n\\nThe desktop experience is the priority because this is a Datathon command-center application.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 26. UX RULE\\n\\nAt any point, ask:\\n\\n\\\"If a judge sees this screen for 5 seconds, can they understand what SKYGUARD XAI is doing?\\\"\\n\\nIf the answer is NO:\\n\\nredesign that section.\\n\\nThe user should NOT have to:\\n\\n- inspect the map\\n- open multiple menus\\n- understand orbital terminology\\n- interpret raw telemetry\\n- guess why something is red\\n\\nThe UI must explain the intelligence.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 27. DO NOT FAKE DATA\\n\\nVery important:\\n\\nDo not create fake AI outputs just to make the UI look impressive.\\n\\nUse the backend's actual:\\n\\n- telemetry\\n- anomaly\\n- risk\\n- confidence\\n- conjunction\\n- event\\n- timestamp\\n\\nIf something is simulated, clearly label it:\\n\\nSIMULATED DATA\\n\\nIf something is real:\\n\\nREAL DATA\\n\\nDo not claim operational aerospace accuracy.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 28. IBM Z CONNECTION VISIBILITY\\n\\nDo not put a huge fake \\\"IBM Z CONNECTED\\\" badge on the dashboard.\\n\\nIf IBM Z integration is not actually connected:\\n\\nshow:\\n\\nTRANSACTIONAL INTELLIGENCE\\nIntegration Ready\\n\\nor:\\n\\nIBM Z Adapter\\nREADY\\n\\nDo not falsely claim live IBM Z connectivity.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 29. DESIGN STYLE\\n\\nThe design should feel like:\\n\\nNASA-style mission operations\\n+\\nmodern AI observability\\n+\\nreal-time financial risk dashboard\\n+\\nemergency command center\\n\\nBut do NOT copy any specific company's interface.\\n\\nUse:\\n\\n- dark professional command-center theme if compatible with existing project\\n- clean typography\\n- strong information hierarchy\\n- subtle borders\\n- restrained glow/effects\\n- clear status indicators\\n- readable charts\\n- meaningful animations\\n\\nAvoid:\\n\\n- excessive neon\\n- excessive gradients\\n- random glassmorphism\\n- giant decorative planets\\n- unnecessary 3D objects\\n- excessive animations\\n- generic dashboard cards\\n- decorative space backgrounds that reduce readability\\n\\nThe application should feel like a serious intelligence platform.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 30. ANIMATION\\n\\nUse animation only when it communicates real-time change.\\n\\nExamples:\\n\\nNew telemetry event:\\nsubtle update\\n\\nRisk:\\n62% → 89%\\n\\nAlert:\\nslide/fade in\\n\\nSatellite:\\nposition update\\n\\nConjunction:\\nvisual connection appears\\n\\nDo NOT animate everything.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 31. FINAL MAIN DASHBOARD\\n\\nThe final dashboard should approximately communicate:\\n\\n============================================================\\nSKYGUARD XAI                         ● LIVE\\nReal-Time Space Intelligence\\n============================================================\\n\\n🚨 HIGH SPACE RISK DETECTED\\n\\nSAT-1042\\n\\nTelemetry anomaly + conjunction risk detected.\\n\\nRisk       Confidence       Health\\n89%        94%              82%\\n\\n------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\nWHAT AI DETECTED\\n\\nTemperature     24°C → 71°C\\nPower           62 → 94\\nBattery         91% → 42\\nSignal          96% → 61%\\n\\n[ HIGH ANOMALY ]\\n\\n------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\n        SPACE MAP              LIVE EVENTS\\n\\n       🛰 SAT-1042             10:02:16\\n          |                    CONJUNCTION\\n          | 0.72 km             DETECTED\\n          |\\n       ○ DEB-2098              10:02:14\\n                               ANOMALY\\n                               DETECTED\\n\\n------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\nWHY IS THIS RISKY?\\n\\n✓ Temperature anomaly\\n✓ Power consumption increase\\n✓ Battery degradation\\n✓ Conjunction detected\\n\\n------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\nSPACE RISK\\n\\nTelemetry Anomaly       HIGH\\nSatellite Health       WARNING\\nConjunction             HIGH\\n\\nOVERALL\\n89%\\n\\n------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\nRECOMMENDED NEXT STEP\\n\\nOperator review required.\\n\\nNo autonomous spacecraft action is performed.\\n\\n============================================================\\n\\nThis is the target experience.\\n\\n============================================================\\n32. IMPLEMENTATION PROCESS\\n============================================================\\n\\nFirst inspect the existing frontend.\\n\\nThen:\\n\\nPHASE 1\\nAudit current UI\\n\\nPHASE 2\\nCreate new information hierarchy\\n\\nPHASE 3\\nBuild dashboard layout\\n\\nPHASE 4\\nConnect actual backend data\\n\\nPHASE 5\\nBuild real-time event updates\\n\\nPHASE 6\\nBuild satellite detail\\n\\nPHASE 7\\nBuild alerts/events pages\\n\\nPHASE 8\\nAdd loading/error/empty states\\n\\nPHASE 9\\nRun demo scenario\\n\\nPHASE 10\\nPolish visual design\\n\\nDo NOT rebuild the backend unless a frontend-required API is genuinely missing.\\n\\nIf an API is missing, first identify the exact required contract.\\n\\n============================================================\\n33. FINAL ACCEPTANCE TEST\\n============================================================\\n\\nOpen SKYGUARD XAI.\\n\\nWithout reading documentation, a new person should be able to answer:\\n\\n1. What is this?\\n2. Is the system live?\\n3. How many satellites are being monitored?\\n4. Is there currently a problem?\\n5. Which satellite is affected?\\n6. What did the AI detect?\\n7. Something is wrong,”** NEXUS-Z does this:\\n\\n**Live Data → AI Detection → Risk Prediction → Impact Analysis → What-If Simulation → Recommended Action**\\n\\nFor example:\\n\\n\u003e 🛰️ Satellite anomaly detected  \\n\u003e ↓  \\n\u003e 🤖 AI determines **89% anomaly probability**  \\n\u003e ↓  \\n\u003e ⚠️ Risk is calculated  \\n\u003e ↓  \\n\u003e 🌍 System checks possible Earth/infrastructure impact  \\n\u003e ↓  \\n\u003e 🔄 Simulates different responses  \\n\u003e ↓  \\n\u003e 💡 Recommends the safest feasible action  \\n\u003e ↓  \\n\u003e 👤 Human reviews and approves\\n\\n### What makes it different?\\n\\nThe main idea is **“don't just detect the problem—understand what happens next.”**\\n\\nIt connects:\\n\\n- **Space:** satellites, telemetry, debris, collision risk\\n- **Earth:** hazards and affected areas\\n- **Infrastructure:** roads, facilities, hospitals\\n- **AI:** anomaly detection, prediction, risk scoring\\n- **Decision intelligence:** what-if scenarios and recommendations\\n- **IBM Z:** real-time/transactional processing close to critical operational data\\n\\n### Why it fits the Datathon\\n\\nThe IBM Z theme is about **making decisions while events are happening, rather than analyzing them afterward**. The project specification describes this same real-time principle: detect risks, predict outcomes, and recommend actions as events occur. fileciteturn0file0L101-L118\\n\\n**One-line explanation pipeline?\\\", \\\"Data quality, model version, latency and audit information\\\"],\\n    ],\\n    [40*mm,57*mm,73*mm]\\n))\\nS.append(P(\\\"1.5 What makes it different?\\\", \\\"H2z\\\"))\\nfor x in [\\n    \\\"\u003cb\u003eIt connects space and Earth.\u003c/b\u003e Satellite information is treated as an input to a larger decision chain.\\\",\\n    \\\"\u003cb\u003eIt predicts consequences.\u003c/b\u003e The system asks what the event may cause, not only whether an event exists.\\\",\\n    \\\"\u003cb\u003eIt simulates choices.\u003c/b\u003e Operators can compare actions \\\"\u003cb\u003eIt is IBM Z aligned.\u003c/b\u003e Critical event processing and AI inference can be placed close to transactional enterprise data.\\\"\\n]:\\n People often have to connect the dots themselves. The result can be slow decisions, incomplete understanding of impact, or action taken after the best response window has already passed.\\\",\\n    RED\\n))\\nS.append(P(\\n    \\\"Example: a remote-sensing system detects conditions consistent with a developing wildfire. Knowing “there is a fire” is useful, but an emergency team needs more: Which roads may become unsafe? How many people may be exposed? Which hospital has capacity? Where should emergency resources be positioned? What happens if the fire expands by another 20%?\\\",\\n    \\\"Bodyz\\\"\\n))\\nS.append(P(\\\"2.2 Why existing solutions are not enough\\\", \\\"H2z\\\"))\\nS.append(make_table(\\n    [\\\"Gap\\\", latency.\\\",\\n    \\\"Add authentication, audit logging and failure handling.\\\",\\n    \\\"Run the full end-to-end demo and benchmark it.\\\"\\n]\\nfor i, x in enumerate(steps, 1):\\n    S.append(numbered(i, x))\\nS.append(PageBreak())\\n\\n# ---------- 7 detailed ----------\\nS.append(P(\\\"7.3 Telemetry anomaly module\\\", \\\"H2z\\\"))\\nS.append(P(\\\"Implementation logic:\\\", \\\"H3z\\\"))\\nfor x in [\\n    \\\"Load recent telemetry for a satellite.\\\",\\n    \\\"Normalize numeric measurements to known units.\\\",\\n    \\\"Calculate rolling mean, rolling standard deviation, slope and deviation from expected operating range.\\\",\\n    \\\"Keep a missing-value flag for every sensor rather than silently hiding missing data.\\\",\\n    \\\"Run Isolation Forest or a robust statistical baseline.\\\",\\n    \\\"Convert the anomaly score to a bounded risk score.\\\",\\n    \\\"Reduce confidence when data is stale, sparse or contradictory.\\\",\\n    \\\"Persist the model version and feature version with the result.\\\"\\n]:\\n    S.append(bullet(x))\\nS.append(P(\\\"Output object:\\\", \\\"H3z\\\"))\\nS.append(diagram(\\\"\\\"\\\"{\\n  \\\"entity_id\\\": \\\"SAT-1042\\\",\\n  \\\"risk_type\\\": \\\"health\\\",\\n  \\\"risk_score\\\": 0.89,\\n  \\\"confidence\\\": 0.91,\\n  \\\"status\\\": \\\"HIGH\\\",\\n  \\\"top_evidence\\\": [\\n    \\\"solar_output_drop\\\",\\n    \\\"thermal_index_rise\\\",\\n    \\\"battery_voltage_deviation\\\"\\n  ],\\n  \\\"model_version\\\": \\\"health-1.0\\\"\\n}\\\"\\\"\\\"))\\nS.append(P(\\\"7.4 Conjunction / collision module\\\", \\\"H2z\\\"))\\nfor x in [\\n    \\\"Read the current state of the satellite and candidate space object.\\\",\\n    \\\"Normalize both states to a common reference time.\\\",\\n    \\\"Propagate states across a defined prediction horizon.\\\",\\n    \\\"Find time of closest approach (TCA).\\\",\\n    \\\"Calculate miss distance and use available uncertainty information.\\\",\\n    \\\"Return a risk estimate; do not describe it as certainty.\\\",\\n    \\\"Generate candidate maneuvers subject to explicit fuel and mission constraints.\\\",\\n    \\\"Compare candidate maneuvers rather than returning an automatic command.\\\"\\n]:\\n    S.append(bullet(x))\\nS.append(callout(\\n    \\\"SAFETY RULE\\\",\\n    \\\"For a hackathon prototype, the maneuver output is a simulated recommendation only. The system must not issue real spacecraft commands. The UI should clearly label it as a proposed maneuver requiring authorized human review.\\\",\\n    RED\\n))\\nS.append(P(\\\"7.5 Earth hazard module\\\", \\\"H2z\\\"))\\nS.append(P(\\n    \\\"For the MVP, choose one hazard. Wildfire is a good demonstration because it is visually understandable and can combine remote sensing with weather and geography. Flood is another good choice because road and hospital impacts are easy to explain.\\\",\\n    \\\"Bodyz\\\"\\n))\\nfor x in [\\n    \\\"Ingest a prepared observation or simulation event.\\\",\\n    \\\"Create or load hazard geometry.\\\",\\n    \\\"Attach probability, severity and observation time.\\\",\\n    \\\"Intersect the hazard geometry with population and road layers.\\\",\\n    \\\"Mark critical facilities that may be exposed or become difficult to reach.\\\",\\n    \\\"Publish a hazard update event to the impact engine.\\\"\\n]:\\n    S.append(bullet(x))\\nS.append(PageBreak())\\n\\n# ---------- 7 cascade/decision ----------\\nS.append(P(\\\"7.6 Cascade impact engine\\\", \\\"H2z\\\"))\\nS.append(P(\\n    \\\"Represent dependencies as a graph. Each edge says how one condition affects another. Keep the relationship rule visible and versioned.\\\",\\n    \\\"Bodyz\\\"\\n))\\nS.append(diagram(\\\"\\\"\\\"Wildfire\\n   ├── affects → road accessibility\\n   │                  └── changes → travel time\\n   ├── affects → population exposure\\n   └── increases → emergency demand\\n                         └── loads → hospital capacity\\n\\nFlood\\n   ├── closes → road segment\\n   ├── exposes → population\\n   └── reduces → facility accessibility\\\"\\\"\\\"))\\nS.append(P(\\\"7.7 What-if engine\\\", \\\"H2z\\\"))\\nfor x in [\\n    \\\"Take an immutable baseline state.\\\",\\n    \\\"Apply scenario assumptions in memory or to a temporary state.\\\",\\n    \\\"Run the same impact models used for the baseline.\\\",\\n    \\\"Calculate outputs such as exposure, travel time, capacity and risk.\\\",\\n    \\\"Store the scenario assumptions and model versions.\\\",\\n    \\\"Never modify live operational state during simulation.\\\"\\n]:\\n    S.append(bullet(x))\\nS.append(P(\\\"Example scenario:\\\", \\\"H3z\\\"))\\nS.append(diagram(\\\"\\\"\\\"BASELINE:\\nFlood area = 100 units\\nHospital A load = 82%\\nRoad R1 = OPEN\\n\\nSCENARIO:\\nFlood area = +20%\\nAction = close R1 + move ambulances to Zone B\\n\\nRESULT:\\nHospital A load = 74%\\nEstimated response time = -9 min\\nPopulation exposure = -27%\\n\\nRECOMMENDATION:\\nScenario is preferred if action is authorized.\\\"\\\"\\\"))\\nS.append(P(\\\"7.8 Decision engine\\\", \\\"H2z\\\"))\\nS.append(P(\\n    \\\"The decision engine must be deterministic enough that the same inputs and model versions produce the same ranking. Use the LLM after this stage, not before it.\\\",\\n    \\\"Bodyz\\\"\\n))\\nS.append(diagram(\\\"\\\"\\\"score(action) =\\n  + risk_reduction\\n  + human_impact_reduction\\n  + response_speed\\n  - resource_cost\\n  - mission_disruption\\n  - uncertainty_penalty\\n\\nHARD RULES:\\n  safety_ok == TRUE\\n  authorization_valid == TRUE\\n  required_data_quality == TRUE\\n\\nIf any hard rule fails:\\n  action = NOT_ALLOWED\\\"\\\"\\\"))\\nS.append(PageBreak())\\n\\n# ---------- 7 API/error ----------\\nS.append(P(\\\"7.9 API design\\\", \\\"H2z\\\"))\\nS.append(make_table(\\n \\\"/api/v1/hazards\\\", \\\"Current hazard states.\\\"],\\n        [\\\"GET\\\", \\\"/api/v1/impacts/{hazard_id}\\\", \\\"Affected people/assets.\\\"],\\n        [\\\"POST\\\", \\\"/api/v1/scenarios/{id}/results\\\", \\\"Get scenario comparison.\\\"],\\n        [\\\"POST\\\", \\\"/api/v1/recommendations/generate\\\", \\\"Rank candidate actions.\\\"],\\n        [\\\"POST\\\", \\\"/api/v1/recommendations/{id}/approve\\\", \\\"Record human approval.\\\"],\\n        [\\\"GET\\\", \\\"/api/v1/audit/{id}\\\", \\\"Retrieve decision evidence.\\\"],\\n        [\\\"GET\\\", \\\"/api/v1/health\\\", \\\"Service health check.\\\"],\\n    ],\\n    [20*mm,75*mm,75*mm]\\n))\\nS.append(P(\\\"7.10 Example API payloads\\\", \\\"H2z\\\"))\\nS.append(P(\\\"Create scenario:\\\", \\\"H3z\\\"))\\nS.append(diagram(\\\"\\\"\\\"POST /api/v1/scenarios\\n\\n{\\n  \\\"baseline_id\\\": \\\"hazard-77\\\",\\n  \\\"assumptions\\\": {\\n    \\\"hazard_extent_multiplier\\\": 1.20\\n  },\\n  \\\"actions\\\": [\\n    {\\\"id\\\": \\\"A\\\", \\\"type\\\": \\\"NO_ACTION\\\"},\\n    {\\\"id\\\": \\\"B\\\", \\\"type\\\": \\\"CLOSE_ROAD\\\", \\\"road_id\\\": \\\"R17\\\"},\\n    {\\\"id\\\": \\\"C\\\", \\\"type\\\": \\\"RELOCATE_RESOURCE\\\", \\\"resource_id\\\": \\\"AMB-4\\\",\\n     \\\"destination\\\": \\\"ZONE-B\\\"}\\n  ]\\n}\\\"\\\"\\\"))\\nS.append(P(\\\"Recommendation \\\"requires_human_approval\\\": true,\\n  \\\"model_versions\\\": {\\n    \\\"impact\\\": \\\"impact-1.0\\\",\\n    \\\"decision\\\": \\\"decision-1.0\\\"\\n  }\\n}\\\"\\\"\\\"))\\nS.append(P(\\\"7.11 Error handling and edge cases\\\", \\\"H2z\\\"))\\nS.append(make_table(\\n    [\\\"Case\\\", \\\"What can go wrong\\\", \\\"Required mitigation\\\"],\\n    [\\n        [\\\"Duplicate event\\\", \\\"Risk is counted twice.\\\", \\\"Idempotency key and deduplication.\\\"],\\n        [\\\"Out-of-order event\\\", \\\"Old state overwrites newer state.\\\", \\\"Event-time processing and version checks.\\\"],\\n        [\\\"Missing telemetry\\\", \\\"Model appears more confident than it should.\\\", \\\"Missingness flags + confidence reduction.\\\"],\\n        [\\\"Stale data\\\", \\\"Recommendation is based on old conditions.\\\", \\\"TTL checks and visible data age.\\\"],\\n        [\\\"Conflicting sources\\\", \\\"Two systems disagree.\\\", \\\"Source priority + freshness + reconciliation.\\\"],\\n        [\\\"Model unavailable\\\", \\\"Pipeline stops.\\\", \\\"Fallback rule/model + degraded-mode banner.\\\"],\\n        [\\\"LLM unavailable\\\", \\\"Explanation cannot be generated.\\\", \\\"Show deterministic evidence and recommendation.\\\"],\\n        [\\\"LLM hallucination\\\", \\\"Explanation contains unsupported calculations.\\\"],\\n        [\\\"Unsafe action\\\", \\\"Bad recommendation reaches operator.\\\", \\\"Hard constraints + approval gate.\\\"],\\n    ],\\n    [37*mm,55*mm,78*mm]\\n))\\nS.append(PageBreak())\\n\\n# ---------- 8 ----------\\nS.append(P(\\\"8. What you pasted is actually a **very strong direction**, but I would simplify it before your team commits.\\n\\nThe biggest opportunity is to make the project **not just “space monitoring” but a real-time cascading decision system**:\\n\\n\u003e 🛰️ **Something happens in space → AI understands it → predicts consequences on Earth → simulates possible actions → recommends the best action.**\\n\\nThat is much more distinctive than a standard satellite-anomaly project.\\n\\n## The one thing I'd change\\n\\nYour current architecture has **too many independent AI problems**:\\n\\n- ↓\\nRecommended Action\\n       ↓\\nBLOCK / REVIEW / ALLOW\\n```\\n\\n**That is exactly what this theme is asking for.**\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 🚨 But don't build a basic fraud detector\\n\\nThis is important.\\n\\nThe organizers themselves mention:\\n\\n\u003e “Detect suspicious transactions before they result in financial loss.”\\n\\nSo if 50 teams build:\\n\\n\u003e **Credit-card fraud
